2025-06-18T11:24:35.1942492Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1942630Z <pr_title>
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1943221Z Simplify CI: consolidate bloated workflows and add immediate wheel access
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1943695Z </pr_title>
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1943891Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1944052Z <pr_description>
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1945341Z This PR addresses the CI complexity and reliability issues by dramatically simplifying the workflow structure and providing multiple access methods for package installation.
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1946819Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1946966Z ## Problem
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1947505Z The repository had **6 CI workflow files** with significant redundancy:
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1948005Z - `build-artifacts.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1948307Z - `build-packages-dev.yaml` 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1948744Z - `build-packages-prod.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1949556Z - `build-packages-staging.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1950059Z - `create-release.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1950483Z - `tools.yaml`
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1950705Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1951734Z This created maintenance complexity and CI reliability issues, making it difficult to successfully build and access wheel artifacts.
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1952737Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1952885Z ## Solution
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1953075Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1953251Z ### 1. Consolidated CI Workflows
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1953813Z **Reduced from 6 to 3 focused workflows** (50% reduction):
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1954264Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1954962Z - **`build-and-deploy.yaml`**: Unified workflow that builds once and deploys to appropriate environments based on triggers
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1956599Z - **`commit-wheels.yaml`**: Fallback workflow that commits wheels directly to the repository when CI builds succeed
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1957740Z - **`tools.yaml`**: Unchanged, handles build tools and container management
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1958281Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1958463Z ### 2. Multiple Installation Methods
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1958768Z Users can now install packages via:
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1958955Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1959057Z **One-line installer:**
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1959281Z ```bash
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1959808Z curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/install.sh | bash -s -- pandas
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1960527Z ```
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1960637Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1960733Z **Direct wheel download:**
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1960970Z ```bash
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1961648Z curl -L https://raw.githubusercontent.com/sgbaird/opentrons-python-packages/main/wheels/pandas-1.5.0-cp310-cp310-linux_armv7l.whl -o /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1971332Z pip install /tmp/pandas.whl
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1971614Z ```
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1971729Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1971892Z **CI artifacts and releases** (when CI runs successfully)
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1972174Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1972279Z ### 3. Immediate Package Access
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1972921Z Added pre-built wheel files directly to the repository in the `wheels/` directory, so packages are immediately available even if CI fails.
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1973466Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1973559Z ## Key Benefits
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1973825Z - **Simplified maintenance**: 50% fewer workflow files
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1974349Z - **Eliminated redundancy**: Single build logic instead of duplicated across multiple files
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1974895Z - **Improved reliability**: Multiple fallback access methods
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1975400Z - **Better user experience**: Immediate access to packages via curl/pip as requested
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1976174Z - **Clear documentation**: Added `CI-SIMPLIFICATION.md` explaining the changes
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1976520Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1976909Z The solution directly addresses the "bloated" CI concern while ensuring reliable package access through multiple channels.
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1977410Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1977496Z Fixes #11.
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1977605Z 
2025-06-18T11:24:35.1977609Z

2025-06-18T11:27:53.3740129Z   -

2025-06-18T11:27:53.3742374Z   -## Problem
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3742685Z   -
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3743301Z   -Prefect 3.3.4 depends on `pendulum>=3.0.0,<4` for date/time operations. However, pendulum:
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3744258Z   -
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3745159Z   -1. **Uses Rust compilation**: Pendulum 3.x is built with maturin and includes Rust extensions for performance
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3746783Z   -2. **ARM compilation challenges**: Building pendulum for ARM7HF (OT-2 architecture) is complex due to Rust cross-compilation requirements
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3748377Z   -3. **Import failures**: Even when hatchling and other dependencies are resolved, missing pendulum causes Prefect import failures
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3749366Z   -
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3749649Z   -## Solution
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3749952Z   -
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3750386Z   -This repository now provides **two pendulum wheel options**:
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3750965Z   -
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3751345Z   -### 1. Universal Wheel (Recommended for compatibility)
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3751979Z   -- **File**: `pendulum-3.1.0-py3-none-any.whl` (109KB)
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3752662Z   -- **Architecture**: Platform-independent, pure Python fallback
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3753432Z   -- **Compatibility**: Works on any Python 3.9+ system including OT-2
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3754284Z   -- **Performance**: Slightly slower than compiled version but fully functional
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3754962Z   -
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3755314Z   -### 2. ARM64 Optimized Wheel (Best performance)
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3756270Z   -- **File**: `pendulum-3.1.0-cp310-cp310-manylinux_2_17_aarch64.manylinux2014_aarch64.whl` (336KB)
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3757136Z   -- **Architecture**: Compiled for ARM64/aarch64 systems
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3757928Z   -- **Compatibility**: May work on OT-2 depending on glibc version and ABI compatibility
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3758751Z   -- **Performance**: Full native performance with Rust optimizations
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3759120Z   -
2025-06-18T11:27:53.3759303Z   -## Installation
